Source URL: https://kenyonexpress.co.il/product/%D7%A7%D7%95%D7%A4%D7%95%D7%9F-%D7%98%D7%A1%D7%98/

Page title: קופון טסט

Viewport: 1440x900

Captured: 2026-07-26

Live coupon PDP used for this capture: `קופון טסט`.

Pricing copy on this page (visible in `.summary`):

- `מחיר רגיל: ₪100`
- `מחיר בקניון: ₪50`
- WooCommerce `.price` shown to cart: `₪9`
- Location meta: `עִיר: Israel`, `אֵזוֹר: tel-aviv:תל אביב,`

Note: live does not use the strings "לתשלום באתר" / "בבית העסק" on this PDP.
The dual-price language present is `מחיר רגיל` vs `מחיר בקניון`.
Related product cards also expose `.custom-price-wrapper > .full-price` + `.discount-price`.

Values from `getComputedStyle` plus `getBoundingClientRect` (`--rect-*`).
`--text` is the visible text snippet (not a CSS property).
